# A13 live test-battery evidence (2026-07-04, this session — all measured, none inferred)

Run against the deployed stack during a controlled test window: `allUsers` invoker REMOVED first
(public probes 403 — zero public exposure; NB this also takes api.khelsutra.guru down for testers
during the window since CF rides that binding), then edge-auth-off revisions; restored to rev 00010
(digest `31475f27…`, auth ON, allUsers re-added) and posture re-verified at the end:
health(token)=200, health(public)=200 (exempt), process-sans-JWT=401, api.khelsutra.guru=302,
gemini_key_present=false.

## Failure-mode battery (all via identity token, rev 00008)

| Probe | Result |
|---|---|
| process gs://evil-bucket/x.mp4 (foreign bucket) | **400** `hosted mode: '…' is not under the allowed input root 'gs://khelsutra-rally-serving/videos'` (#465 live at the API surface) |
| process gs://khelsutra-rally-serving/videos-evil/x.mp4 (separator-boundary attack) | **400** same jail message (the boundary check holds) |
| process nonexistent object under input_root | **202** job queued → job `failed`, error `NotFound: 404 … No such object: khelsutra-rally-serving/videos/definitely-not-here.mp4` (clean async failure, client-visible via poll) |
| process bad segmenter | **400** `Segmenter 'not_a_segmenter' not found. Available segmenters: […]` |
| GET /api/jobs/deadbeef | **404** |
| compile bad video_id | **404** `Indexed video record not found.` |
| GET /api/highlights/x/..%2F..%2Fconfig.json (traversal) | **404** (rejected; no file disclosure) |
| GET /api/highlights/<missing>/reel.mp4 | **404** |
| upload init filename=malware.exe | **400** `Extension '.exe' not allowed. Allowed: ['mov', 'mp4']` |
| upload init total_size 99999999999 | **413** `File exceeds the 4096 MB upload limit.` |
| upload PUT part/5 first (out-of-order) | **409** `Out-of-order part: expected 0, got 5` ; DELETE cleanup → 200 |

## Concurrency smoke (#468 live, rev 00008)

3 distinct 720p clips (4s/5s/6s) uploaded + submitted `compute=cloud` back-to-back → ALL THREE jobs
claimed and completed `success` (0 segments each — synthetic content). No false-empty, no stranded
job, GPU-quota=1 serialized the L4 executions but the drain claimed everything.

## Gemini handoff leg (rev 00009 — one-off test config, NOT the alpha posture; restored after)

- Prep finding: the `gemini-api-key` secret existed but had **NO accessor grant** — the documented
  secret-mount path had never been exercised. Fixed live:
  `gcloud secrets add-iam-policy-binding gemini-api-key --member=serviceAccount:492532266377-compute@developer.gserviceaccount.com --role=roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor`
  (grant left in place; the secret is only mounted when a config asks for it).
- Job: `gcloud run jobs update rally-worker --set-secrets GEMINI_API_KEY=gemini-api-key:latest` — worked.
- CP: manifest with `skip_ai_handoff=false` + secret env → booted clean (`AI_HANDOFF_KEY_MISSING`
  startup check passed; `gemini_key_present=true` in /api/capabilities).
- Run: MahadevpuraSingles, wasb_hybrid, compute=cloud → **success, 43 play segments** (vs 48 in
  WASB-only mode — the AI pass genuinely refined/filtered).
- Worker logs prove real Gemini traffic: `backend.providers.gemini [Gemini Handoff 1..48] Submitting
  multimodal request to gemini-flash-latest` → `POST https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/
  models/gemini-flash-latest:generateContent "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"` for all 48 windows; `Phase 3
  completed. AI confirmed 43 semantic play segments.`; execution `rally-worker-khx58`, wall ~similar
  to WASB-only + ~4-11s per handoff window.
- Restored after: secret unmounted from the job (`--remove-secrets`), alpha config back
  (skip_ai_handoff=true), `gemini_key_present=false` re-verified.

## Other live facts gathered today

- The CP serves the bundled SPA at its root: `GET /` → 200 `text/html` (single-origin — the tester
  path has NO cross-origin CORS dependency; the CF-Access "bypass OPTIONS" concern applies only to a
  split-origin deploy).
- Domain-mapping cert (issue #472): `Ready;CertificateProvisioned;DomainRoutable = True;True;True`
  today (2026-07-04) — healthy; the ~90-day renewal watch date stands (~2026-09-30).
- Better auth-off window practice (used today, recommend for the runbook): REMOVE the allUsers
  invoker for the duration of any edge-auth-off window (public 403; identity tokens still work),
  re-add on restore. Cost: api.khelsutra.guru is down for testers during the window.
- khelsutra copy launch-blockers filed as khelsutra issue #154 (SetupWizard "never leaves your
  device" false on hosted; policy "our own infrastructure" on GCP; SPA has zero consent copy).

## 0. TL;DR + recommendation

The A1 stack is live and end-to-end green on the final image (48-segment CUJ, signed-URL download, edge auth restored — e2e runlog §3/§6), and this session's live battery adds: the hosted-mode input jail holds at the API surface against foreign-bucket and separator-boundary attacks, upload/compile misuse fails with clean 4xx, a missing gs:// object fails cleanly and client-visibly, three concurrent cloud jobs all completed with no stranded state, and the domain-mapping cert is healthy today. Auth posture, rollback levers, and data-deletion mechanics are documented below. Training capture is hard-denied in code — stronger than the privacy policy promises.

What is **not** done: nobody has ever completed the CUJ **in a browser through Cloudflare Access** (both runlogs and the battery drove it with curl + tokens/JWTs — the tracker's own DoD #1 is unchecked); the Studio SPA's first-run wizard tells testers *"your videos never leave this device"*, which is **false** on the hosted alpha, and the public privacy policy says footage is processed on *"our own infrastructure"* while it actually runs on Google Cloud in Singapore (both filed as **khelsutra #154**); A10 (ship-gate target) and A11 (WASB-C3 provenance) remain owner-gated, so **no quality or owned-model claims are allowed in any copy**; the runtime service account is over-privileged for a public-invoker service; and retention/deletion is manual-only (#301, #447).

**Recommendation (the decision is the owner's — this is the evidence-supported shape):**

- **Conditional GO for the CURRENT 4-email allowlist**, with two conditions to clear **before testers touch it**:
  1. Fix the khelsutra copy blockers (**khelsutra #154**): suppress/fix the SetupWizard "never leaves this device" claim for the hosted build, correct the policy's "our own infrastructure" line, and add minimum upload-moment disclosure (§4.4 draft copy is ready to ship).
  2. Owner completes the **5-minute browser-CUJ checklist** (§1.1) — closing the tracker's DoD #1 with a real browser pass.
  Rationale: the blast radius is 4 known, owner-invited people; the stack, auth, failure modes, and rollback are verified; the two conditions are the only tester-facing falsehoods/unknowns left. Recording GO also requires the owner to **explicitly defer A11** with the documented "no commercial model sharing" copy guardrail (`docs/ALPHA_LAUNCH_READINESS.md:110`) — the current testers are already non-owner users, which sits in tension with the Dockerfile's "no public, non-owner serving" warning (`deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile:14-16`); that tension must be owned, not implied.
- **NO-GO for any wider invite wave** until: the owner closes **A10** (ship-gate target + allowed claims) and **A11** (WASB-C3 resolve-or-defer), the runtime SA is replaced with a **least-privilege SA** (§5.5 — today it holds project `roles/editor` on a service with an `allUsers` invoker), and the **retention/deletion over-promise** is addressed (automation via #301 or softened policy wording). The no-content-screening gap (#332) is acceptable at 4 trusted users and not beyond.

## 1. Scorecard — the tracker's Definition of done, mapped to evidence

Against `docs/ALPHA_LAUNCH_READINESS.md` §10 (lines 224–235):

**The next stage is done: A13 — the alpha launch review — is delivered and merged ([#478](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/478)), backed by a thorough live test battery on the deployed stack including the Gemini leg. The launch GO/NO-GO now sits with you, with a concrete evidence-based recommendation.**

## How it went

**Live test battery (all measured on the real stack, posture fully restored after):**
- **11-probe failure-mode battery** — the hosted-mode input jail held against a foreign bucket *and* a separator-boundary attack (`videos-evil`); a missing gs:// object fails cleanly and client-visibly; bad segmenter/compile/jobs/traversal all return crisp 4xx; uploads reject `.exe`, enforce the (newly confirmed) **4096 MB cap** with a 413, and 409 out-of-order parts.
- **Concurrency smoke** — three simultaneous cloud jobs all claimed and completed; no false-empty, no stranded dispatch. The #468 fix holds under a real burst.
- **Gemini handoff leg** — the piece the WASB-only alpha never exercised. Two real finds: the `gemini-api-key` secret **had no accessor grant** (the documented mount path had never actually been used — fixed live), and then the full pipeline ran clean: `skip_ai_handoff=false` booted with the key check passing, and the MahadevpuraSingles run produced **43 AI-confirmed segments vs 48 WASB-only**, with worker logs showing real `gemini-flash-latest` calls for all 48 handoff windows. The Gemini path *works* in the hosted stack; the alpha stays WASB-only by design, and I restored that posture (rev `00010`, auth ON, `gemini_key_present=false` re-verified, `allUsers` restored for the CF path).
- Security hygiene: during test windows I removed the `allUsers` invoker first, so nothing was ever publicly exposed (note: that briefly takes `api.khelsutra.guru` down for testers — now documented as the standard practice).

**The review itself** (`docs/ALPHA_LAUNCH_REVIEW_2026-07-04.md`, 475 lines, built from a 4-agent research fan-out + adversarial critique + the battery evidence, scrubbed of secrets/emails):
- **Recommendation: conditional GO for the current 4-email allowlist** — with two conditions before testers touch it — and **NO-GO for any wider invites** until A10 (ship-gate) and A11 (WASB-C3 provenance) close, plus a least-privilege service account and a retention fix.
- **The two conditions:** (1) fix the copy blockers filed as [khelsutra #154](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/issues/154) — the Studio wizard tells users their video "never leaves this device," which is **false** on the hosted alpha, and the policy claims "our own infrastructure" while it runs on Google Cloud Singapore; (2) run the 5-minute **browser CUJ checklist** in the review's §1.1 — the honest gap the critique surfaced is that every CUJ pass so far (mine included) was curl-driven, and the tracker's DoD #1 requires a real browser pass, which only an allowlisted human can do.
- Also inside: a full operator runbook (with the deploy gotchas and cost notes), an observability/triage table built from the actual battery results, the tester one-pager, rollback levers R1–R5, and the SA blast-radius warning (`roles/editor` on a public-invoker service).

**Budget:** this stage consumed roughly four L4 executions (three short, one full match), one Gemini-enabled run (~48 flash-model calls), and no new image builds — well inside the $300; the exact dollar figure needs a billing-console glance, which the review flags rather than guesses.

**What's on you now:** the GO/NO-GO record, the §1.1 browser run-through, the #154 copy approval, the A10/A11 decisions, and the cert check before ~Sept 30 (healthy as of today). Memory and the handoff are updated so any session can pick up from exactly here; the natural next engineering grab is the khelsutra #154 fix PR.
